## 〇、怎么用这份文件

**它是什么**：一份**可复现的检索留痕**。每条记录都带 arXiv ID / DOI / URL，任何人点开就能核对。

**它不是什么**：

- ❌ **不是 T1 证据**。本项目 T1 = 官方明文（Apple HIG / 固件源码）。一条 arXiv 摘要说明的是「论文声称了什么」，**不等于事实已被独立验证**。
- ❌ 不是综述结论。真正的结论必须回源读原文后**自己写**，本文件只负责「有哪些东西可读」。
- ❌ 不是完整检索。arXiv + S2 只覆盖预印本与部分正式出版物，付费墙内的期刊论文会有缺口。

**引用纪律**：本项目任何一条对外结论，若依据来自本文件，必须回源读原文 → 在本仓库另写一份带 `file:行号` 或 `arXiv ID` 的取证记录 → 才可用于交付文档。

## 二、语音交互延迟与人类对话轮次容忍度

**主题键**：`voice-latency`

**为什么要查**：本项目的陪伴体验成败在「按下麦克风到出声」这段。人类对话里超过某个阈值就会感觉「卡」而不是「慢」。要找的是**可引用的阈值证据**，不是厂商宣传。

**检索式（可复现）**：

- arXiv：`all:"turn taking" AND all:"speech" AND all:"latency"`
- arXiv：`all:"conversational latency"`
- arXiv：`all:"speech" AND all:"response delay" AND all:"user experience"`
- Semantic Scholar：`conversational latency speech turn taking user experience`
- Semantic Scholar：`voice assistant response delay perception`

## 二、流式 TTS 的首包延迟与分段播放

**主题键**：`tts-streaming`

**为什么要查**：决定「小栈开口」要等多久。流式合成 vs 整段合成差一个量级；且分段播放会引入拼接断点。本项目已实测「关键帧驱动切法」（tools/audio_chunk.py），需要文献佐证分段策略的代价。

**检索式（可复现）**：

- arXiv：`all:"text-to-speech" AND all:"streaming" AND all:"latency"`
- arXiv：`all:"streaming speech synthesis"`
- arXiv：`all:"first byte" AND all:"speech synthesis"`
- Semantic Scholar：`streaming text-to-speech first packet latency`
- Semantic Scholar：`chunked speech synthesis concatenation artifacts`

## 二、LLM 陪伴机器人的拟人化、依恋与再互动

**主题键**：`llm-companion`

**为什么要查**：产品定位是「陪伴」。要找的是：拟人化到什么程度会让人愿意反复回来，以及哪些设计会踩「creepy」红线。这是护城河论证（spec/04）的外部证据。

**检索式（可复现）**：

- arXiv：`all:"social robot" AND all:"anthropomorphism"`
- arXiv：`all:"LLM" AND all:"companion" AND all:"attachment"`
- arXiv：`all:"social chatbot" AND all:"anthropomorphic"`
- arXiv：`all:"re-engagement" AND all:"social robot"`
- Semantic Scholar：`LLM companion chatbot anthropomorphism attachment re-engagement`
- Semantic Scholar：`social robot conversational quality re-engagement intention`

## 二、机器人屏幕脸 / 表情渲染与可辨识度

**主题键**：`robot-face`

**为什么要查**：StackChan 的脸是 320×240 单色屏。本项目要决定 App 端的脸是「逐像素镜像设备」还是「风格化」。要找的是：低分辨率、单色条件下，表情的可辨识度受什么约束。

**检索式（可复现）**：

- arXiv：`all:"facial expression" AND all:"robot" AND all:"display"`
- arXiv：`all:"emotive" AND all:"robot face"`
- arXiv：`all:"simplified" AND all:"facial expression" AND all:"recognition"`
- Semantic Scholar：`robot face display emotional expression rendering`
- Semantic Scholar：`low resolution line drawing facial expression recognition`

### robot-face.20 Rethinking the Learning Paradigm for Facial Expression Recognition

- arXiv:`2209.15402` ｜ 日期：2022-09-30 ｜ 出处：arXiv preprint ｜ 来源：arXiv
- 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2209.15402
- 作者：Weijie Wang, Bo Li, Nicu Sebe, Bruno Lepri

> 摘要（原文截断，完整内容请点链接）：
>
> Due to the subjective crowdsourcing annotations and the inherent inter-class similarity of
> facial expressions, the real-world Facial Expression Recognition (FER) datasets usually exhibit
> ambiguous annotation. To simplify the learning paradigm, most previous methods convert ambiguous
> annotation results into precise one-hot annotations and train FER models in an end-to-end
> supervised manner. In this paper, we rethink the existing training paradigm and propose that it
> is better to use weakly supervised strategies to train FER models with original ambiguous
> annotation.

- arXiv：`all:"trust" AND all:"anthropomorphic agent"`

## 二、AI 陪伴的隐私、依赖与伦理争议

**主题键**：`privacy-companion`

**为什么要查**：本项目硬约束是「个人定制数据永不入库」。同时要诚实面对：陪伴型 AI 的依赖风险、情感操纵风险是已知争议。求职作品里这一块写清楚反而加分。

**检索式（可复现）**：

- arXiv：`all:"social robot" AND all:"privacy" AND all:"concerns"`
- arXiv：`all:"AI companion" AND all:"ethics"`
- arXiv：`all:"emotional manipulation" AND all:"chatbot"`
- Semantic Scholar：`domestic social robot privacy security concerns`
- Semantic Scholar：`AI companion emotional dependency ethics`

---

## 二、社交机器人与亲社会行为 / 幸福感效应

**主题键**：`prosocial-robot`

**为什么要查**：护城河论证不能只说「聊天像人」。要找的是**机器人陪伴带来的可测收益**（亲社会行为、孤独感、幸福感）—— 这决定「陪伴型机器人」是不是真需求，还是只是新鲜感。也决定功能取舍该往「让人变好」还是「让人离不开」倾斜。

**检索式（可复现）**：

- arXiv：`all:"social robot" AND all:"prosocial"`
- arXiv：`all:"social robot" AND all:"wellbeing" AND all:"loneliness"`
- arXiv：`all:"robot" AND all:"prosocial behavior" AND all:"children"`
- Semantic Scholar：`social robot prosocial behavior children empathy`
- Semantic Scholar：`social robot loneliness wellbeing older adults intervention`

> its own actions, including helping and signaling. We formalize this as a latent-state POMDP with
> limited observations and learn the transition and observation dynamics using expectation
> maximization. The resulting belief-based policy balances task and social objectives, selecting
> actions that maximize long-term cooperative outcomes. We evaluate the model using data from user
> studies and show that the learned policy …


---

## 二、长期记忆、人格一致性与跨会话连续性

**主题键**：`memory-persona-consistency`

**为什么要查**：本项目的人格包 + 记忆是三层结构，核心主张是「人格跨会话稳定」。要找的是：什么条件下用户能感知到「还是同一个人」、记忆一致性的评价方法、以及长期记忆引入的**矛盾/遗忘**风险。这直接约束记忆合并策略与回滚设计。

**检索式（可复现）**：

- arXiv：`all:"long-term memory" AND all:"conversational agent"`
- arXiv：`all:"persona consistency" AND all:"dialogue"`
- arXiv：`all:"narrative consistency" AND all:"LLM" AND all:"memory"`
- Semantic Scholar：`long-term memory conversational agent persona consistency`
- Semantic Scholar：`LLM chatbot memory consistency evaluation multi-session`

## 二、语音对话的打断处理、重叠语音与轮次管理

**主题键**：`voice-barge-in`

**为什么要查**：iOS 端已定音频 category = Play and record（要同时播和收）。真正的难点是**barge-in**（用户抢话）与**重叠语音**（自己说话时把自己的声音收进来）。要找的是回声消除、VAD 门限、抢话判定阈值的可引用做法 —— 这是 App 侧音频链路的真实风险点，之前只做了 TTS 播放侧，**收音侧还没设计**。

**检索式（可复现）**：

- arXiv：`all:"barge-in" AND all:"voice assistant"`
- arXiv：`all:"speech separation" AND all:"self-echo" AND all:"full-duplex"`
- arXiv：`all:"voice activity detection" AND all:"overlap" AND all:"dialogue"`
- Semantic Scholar：`barge-in voice assistant interruption full duplex speech`
- Semantic Scholar：`acoustic echo cancellation self speech detection microphone`

> 摘要（原文截断，完整内容请点链接）：
>
> Large language models (LLMs), while driving a new wave of interactive AI applications across
> numerous domains, suffer from high inference costs and heavy cloud dependency. Motivated by the
> redundancy phenomenon in linguistics, we propose a progressive inference paradigm over cloud and

## 二、情感计算：语音韵律的情绪特征与识别

**主题键**：`affective-computing`

**为什么要查**：本项目的「韵律 → 动作」链路（tools/prosody_to_motion.py）目前是自研启发式。要找的是学界常用的韵律特征维度（valence/arousal 等）与情绪识别的可靠性边界，以便说明我们抽取的 7 维特征是站在什么基础上、有哪些已知失效模式。

**检索式（可复现）**：

- arXiv：`all:"affective computing" AND all:"speech" AND all:"emotion recognition"`
- arXiv：`all:"prosody" AND all:"emotion" AND all:"acoustic features"`
- arXiv：`all:"valence" AND all:"arousal" AND all:"speech"`
- Semantic Scholar：`affective computing speech emotion recognition valence arousal`
- Semantic Scholar：`speech prosody features emotion detection reliability`

> so on. Instead, each emotion category is projected into a set of nearly independent dimensions,
> named pleasure (or valence), arousal, and dominance, known as PAD. The value of each dimension
> varies from -1 to 1, such that the neutral emotion is in the center with all-zero values.
> Training an emotional continuous text-to-speech (TTS) synthesizer on the independent dimensions
> provides the possibility of emotional speech synthesis with unlimited emotion categories. Our
> end-to-end neural speech synthesizer is based on the well-known Tacotron. Empirically, we have

---

## 二、机器人肢体动作的情绪表达与感染效应

**主题键**：`robot-motion-expression`

**为什么要查**：小栈有舵机（身体），不只有脸（表情）。要找的是：动作幅度/速度与情绪的可辨识度关系，以及运动表达能否放大或干扰面部表情。这是 spec/06「风格化脸是否允许超 40% 面积」和身体动作参数化的直接依据。

**检索式（可复现）**：

- arXiv：`all:"robot motion" AND all:"emotion" AND all:"expression"`
- arXiv：`all:"embodied" AND all:"expression" AND all:"motion generation"`
- arXiv：`all:"social robot" AND all:"gesture" AND all:"engagement"`
- Semantic Scholar：`robot motion emotion expression recognition`
- Semantic Scholar：`embodied agent gesture animation engagement`

## 二、唤醒词、端侧 VAD 与始终在线语音链路

**主题键**：`wake-word-vad`

**为什么要查**：真机语音链路还缺「收音侧」设计：唤醒词、VAD 门限、始终在线的功耗。ESP32 官方固件是否带音频通道尚未验证 ⇒ 若要 App 侧全权收音，唤醒与 VAD 必须在手机上做。找可引用的门限与功耗做法。

**检索式（可复现）**：

- arXiv：`all:"wake word" AND all:"detection" AND all:"on-device"`
- arXiv：`all:"voice activity detection" AND all:"noise" AND all:"robustness"`
- arXiv：`all:"always-on" AND all:"speech" AND all:"power"`
- Semantic Scholar：`wake word detection on-device low power`
- Semantic Scholar：`voice activity detection noise robustness threshold`

> We present `wake-cough', an application of wake-word spotting to coughs using a Resnet50 and the

> monitoring system. Coughs, recorded in a quiet (73$\pm$5 dB) and noisy (34$\pm$17 dB)
> environment, were used to extract i-vectors, x-vectors and d-vectors, used as features to the
> classifiers. The system achieves 90.02\% accuracy when using an MLP to discriminate between 51

---

## 二、参数化 2D 表情动画：情绪强度与表情混合

**主题键**：`parametric-face-animation`

**为什么要查**：App 端脸是参数化模型（CoreProtocol/AvatarModel 的 eyeWeight / rotation / mouth 尺寸等）。要找的是：单色低分辨率下参数化表情的可辨识度上限、情绪强度该映射到哪个参数、表情之间的混合是否会让观众读错。这直接约束我们「几何一致 / 风格化」的边界。

**检索式（可复现）**：

- arXiv：`all:"facial expression" AND all:"parameterization" AND all:"animation"`
- arXiv：`all:"avatar" AND all:"expression" AND all:"parametric"`
- arXiv：`all:"emotion intensity" AND all:"facial expression" AND all:"animation"`
- Semantic Scholar：`parametric facial expression animation emotion intensity`
- Semantic Scholar：`2D avatar expression blending recognizability low resolution`

- 作者：Vinay Bettadapura

> 摘要（原文截断，完整内容请点链接）：
>
> The automatic recognition of facial expressions has been an active research topic since the

---

## 二、local-first 架构、端云协同与离线可用性

**主题键**：`local-first-architecture`

**为什么要查**：硬约束是「个人定制数据永不入库」⇒ 架构上必须 local-first。要找的是 local-first 语料里对同步/冲突/离线降级的共识做法，以及「云端大脑 + 本地身体」这种分裂式架构有没有已知失败模式。这是 spec/07 §3.6 方案 A 的架构正当性来源。

**检索式（可复现）**：

- arXiv：`all:"local-first" AND all:"software" AND all:"collaboration"`
- arXiv：`all:"edge cloud" AND all:"collaboration" AND all:"latency"`
- arXiv：`all:"offline" AND all:"assistant" AND all:"reliability"`
- Semantic Scholar：`local-first software sync conflict offline`
- Semantic Scholar：`edge cloud collaborative inference latency reliability`

> end-user enhances privacy protection, responsiveness, and reliability. Resource-constrained
> end-devices must be carefully managed to meet the latency and energy requirements of
> computationally intensive deep learning services. Collaborative end-edge-cloud computing for
> deep learning provides a range of performance and efficiency that can address application
> requirements through computation offloading. The decision to offload computation is a

## 二、AI 陪伴的留存、习惯养成与长期参与

**主题键**：`companion-retention`

**为什么要查**：产品成败看留存而非首日惊艳。要找的是：什么机制让用户回来（提醒、连续性、成长感、仪式感），以及哪些机制属于「成瘾式留存」（伦理风险，本项目明确不做）。这决定 spec/04 的功能取舍排序。

**检索式（可复现）**：

- arXiv：`all:"chatbot" AND all:"user engagement" AND all:"retention"`
- arXiv：`all:"AI companion" AND all:"habit" AND all:"engagement"`
- arXiv：`all:"social robot" AND all:"long-term engagement" AND all:"home"`
- Semantic Scholar：`chatbot engagement retention long-term usage`
- Semantic Scholar：`AI companion habit formation engagement design`

## 二、LLM 人格控制、角色一致性与角色卡工程

**主题键**：`llm-persona-control`

**为什么要查**：人格包（personas/enfp.json + persona.py）是三层结构的中间层。要找的是：系统提示词/角色卡怎么做才能跨会话稳定、不漂移、不人格分裂；以及「一致性」该怎么量化评测。这直接约束人格层的实现与回归测试。

**检索式（可复现）**：

- arXiv：`all:"persona" AND all:"language model" AND all:"role"`
- arXiv：`all:"character consistency" AND all:"LLM" AND all:"generation"`
- arXiv：`all:"persona" AND all:"controlled generation" AND all:"dialogue"`
- Semantic Scholar：`LLM persona consistent role playing dialogue`
- Semantic Scholar：`character consistency language model evaluation persona drift`

## 附、复现与校验

```powershell
# 列出全部主题
py -3.12 tools/fetch_literature.py --list

# 抓取全部主题并生成本文件
py -3.12 tools/fetch_literature.py --all --out docs/research/68-陪伴机器人文献综述.md

# 校验本文件里的 arXiv ID 是否仍可解析（引用还在不在）
py -3.12 tools/fetch_literature.py --all --out xxx.md --verify-ids

# 离线自检（不联网，CI 用这个）
py -3.12 tools/fetch_literature.py --self-test
```

**已知缺口（诚实声明）**：

- 只覆盖 arXiv + Semantic Scholar 两个源；ACM DL / IEEE / 期刊付费墙内的论文可能缺。
- 检索式是**相关性排序的前 N 条**，不是穷举；`max_results` 可调。
- Semantic Scholar 的引用数会随时间变化 ⇒ **重跑本文件内容会变**。已生成的文件视为快照，日期以文件头「取证时间」为准。
- arXiv API 要求请求间隔 ≥ 3 秒，全量抓取需要数分钟。
